Specifically, we first analyze the sensing system framework, covering concepts, services and tasks, nodes and targets, and scenarios for LAWN sensing. Next, we conduct a comparative analysis of existing sensing techniques from the perspectives of propagation medium, cooperation, methodology, and modality, analyzing their advantages and limitations. Then, we summarize promising future research directions for deployable LAWN sensing systems, covering non-cooperative and cooperative sensing, model-driven and data-driven sensing, and model-and-data-driven multi-modal sensing. Finally, we present a case study of a model-and-data-driven multi-modal method for real-time aerial target sensing. Compared with existing surveys on LAWN or sensing, this article delivers a more comprehensive, targeted review exclusively centered on LAWN sensing.

</details>


### [213] [Measuring the End-to-End Resilience of Application Deployments in Real-World Communication Networks with DRACO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10611)
*Leon Janzen,Matthias Hollick*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRACO框架，用于建模通信网络并测量应用部署的端到端韧性。它支持在任意通信网络上配置应用部署，并评估其可用性、稳健性和韧性。通过在德国和法国建模全国性应用部署展示了其功能范围，证明其与多种公共数据和合成数据的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信网络作为关键基础设施，运营商很少披露相关信息，这使得难以测量部署的端到端韧性，因为需要猜测拓扑结构和所涉系统的可用性。

Method: 使用DRACO框架，该框架用于建模通信网络并测量端到端韧性。允许在任意通信网络上配置应用部署，并评估其可用性、稳健性和韧性。

Result: 通过在德国和法国建模全国性应用部署展示了DRACO的功能范围，突出其兼容多种公共数据来源和合成数据。

Conclusion: DRACO框架能够测量端到端韧性，并且与多样化数据源兼容，在德国和法国的应用部署中得到了验证。

Abstract: Billions of users take Internet connectivity for granted and use it daily to access applications deployed anywhere around the globe. What users perceive as a seamless connection between their end device and the application server involves a complex system of systems operated by various actors. Failures in any of these systems disrupt user-to-application connectivity if the communication network is not resilient. Since communication networks are a critical infrastructure as part of ICT, real-world operators rarely disclose information about them. This makes it difficult to measure the end-to-end resilience of deployments, because it requires guessing topologies and the availability of the involved systems.
  This paper presents DRACO, a framework for modeling communication networks and measuring end-to-end resilience of application deployments. It enables configuring application deployments across arbitrary communication networks and evaluating their availability, robustness, and resilience. We demonstrate the functional scope of DRACO by modeling nationwide application deployments in Germany and France, highlighting its compatibility with a wide variety of public data sources and synthetic data.

</details>


### [214] [User Satisfaction-Aware Resource Allocation with Prospect-Theoretic Utility for Multimedia Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10625)
*Manoj Kumar S,Pranjal Varshney,Avhishek Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于前景理论，提出了一种考虑吞吐量变化不对称效应的多媒体流资源分配策略，并开发了快速数值方法以确定最优阈值，用于实际网络部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资源分配策略主要关注吞吐量、公平性和延迟等指标，但忽略了用户满意度受吞吐量变化影响这一重要因素。根据行为经济学中的前景理论，相同幅度的吞吐量下降比上升引起更强烈的负面感受（损失厌恶）。现有工作虽考虑了吞吐量变化，但未捕捉这种不对称效应对用户满意度的影响。

Method: 1. 提出通用效用度量，同时捕捉平均吞吐量和吞吐量变化对用户满意度的不对称影响；2. 设计资源分配策略，在系统从资源充足转为资源稀缺时减少分配的突变下降，同时尊重不同用户类别的优先级；3. 基于多队列系统的理论分析，提出快速数值方法确定最优阈值，适应网络实例的频繁变化。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，该策略优于现有的公平分配策略。快速数值方法能够有效确定最优阈值，适用于现场部署场景。

Conclusion: 该研究将前景理论引入多媒体流资源分配领域，通过考虑用户满意度的不对称特性，实现了更优的资源分配效果。针对最优阈值依赖网络实例的问题，提出的快速数值方法为实际部署提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional resource allocation policies for multimedia streaming have primarily targeted metrics such as throughput, fairness and delay. However, user satisfaction is known to be strongly influenced by variations in throughput. Although some recent works have considered throughput variations, they overlooked a well-known aspect of human behavior that has reshaped behavioral economics over the past few decades: the same amount of decrease in throughput causes greater annoyance than the satisfaction caused by the increase. Motivated by this insight from the broad field of economics, popularly known as prospect theory, we propose a generic utility (metric) that captures both the average throughput and the asymmetric effect of throughput variations on user satisfaction. Next, we propose a resource allocation policy that reduces abrupt decreases in allocated resources when the system transitions from a resource-rich to resource-scarce state while respecting the priorities of different classes of users. Extensive simulations show that this policy outperforms the existing fair allocation policies. However, the choice of the optimal threshold depends on the network instance, which, in practice, may change frequently. Hence, building on the theoretical analysis of multi-queue systems, we propose fast numerical methods for determining the optimal threshold, which can be used in field deployments.

</details>


### [215] [Conversational Orchestration for Organic 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10714)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM驱动域代理的轻量级去中心化对话式编排框架，用于实现有机6G网络中的跨域服务供给，解决现有方案依赖重型集成架构和深层遥测流水线导致的部署困难和协调开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机6G愿景要求服务供给具备简单操作性、跨独立管理域的可扩展性以及域动态变更（加入/离开）的敏捷性。现有跨域编排方案依赖重型集成架构、多层协调器和深层遥测流水线，阻碍部署并放大协调开销。

Method: 提出轻量级去中心化对话式编排框架：每个域保持自治，代理通过工具观察本地状态、闭环推理、并通过A2A覆盖层与相邻代理交换摘要。采用周期性路由式可达性广告（延迟、瓶颈带宽、计算容量）实现快速可行放置，通过事件驱动请求和协商处理安全重优化、扩展和迁移。部署经验证器自检训练的紧凑推理模型，并通过影子更新进行在线优化。

Result: 仿真表明：控制平面开销随域规模扩展和域加入时保持近线性可控；决策质量鲁棒，包括目标变化后的恢复能力。

Conclusion: 总结未来研究方向：为有机6G实现有原则、安全且不确定性感知的代理式编排。

Abstract: The Organic 6G vision of a network of networks spanning an edge-cloud continuum complemented by non-terrestrial resources requires, to realize its promise, service provisioning that is simple to operate, scalable across independently administered domains, and agile under domain churn (i.e., domains dynamically joining and leaving). Despite advances in cross-domain orchestration, many proposals rely on heavy integration fabrics, multi-layer coordinators, and deep telemetry pipelines that hinder deployability and amplify coordination overhead. We propose a lightweight, decentralized conversational orchestration framework based on Large Language Model (LLM)-driven domain agents. Each domain remains autonomous: an agent observes local state via tools, reasons in a closed loop, and exchanges summaries with neighboring agents over an Agent-to-Agent (A2A) overlay aligned with data-plane coupling. Fast feasible placement is enabled by periodic, routing-like dissemination of reachability advertisements (latency, bottleneck bandwidth, and compute capacity), while safe re-optimization, scaling, and migration are handled through event-driven requests and negotiation. To meet real-time constraints, we deploy a compact reasoning model trained with verifier-based self-verification and periodically refined online via shadow updates. Simulations show manageable, near-linear control-plane overhead as domains scale and during domain joins, and robust decision quality, including recovery after objective changes. We close by outlining future research directions for principled, secure, and uncertainty-aware agentic orchestration in Organic 6G.

</details>


### [216] [Media-over-Multipath-QUIC for Realtime Video Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10741)
*Tanya Shreedhar,Zuji Zhou,Nitinder Mohan,Fernando Kuipers*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出MoMQ扩展，通过利用MoQT协议的元数据信息来优化多路径传输中的实时视频调度，显著降低帧完成时间和播放缓冲区需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多路径传输的技术对实时视频帮助有限，因为调度器只能看到字节层面信息，无法区分关键帧与增强帧的重要性差异，缺乏应用层与传输层之间的语义通信渠道。

Method: 基于已部署的MoQT协议，提出MoMQ扩展，在边缘中继节点设置声明式规则，匹配对象元数据并表达针对不同标记路径的传输偏好，中继机械地评估规则，根据视频语义进行路径决策而不包含视频逻辑。

Result: 在覆盖Starlink和WiFi的实时测试平台上，4条规则将P99.9帧完成时间从最优传输调度器的384.7ms降至114.1ms，减少61.5%的播放缓冲区需求，是唯一满足150ms交互延迟目标的配置。

Conclusion: MoMQ通过利用现有元数据流实现应用语义感知的路径调度，无需修改底层传输协议，同一规则在不同地理区域保持不变并持续有效，证明了元数据驱动的路径优化在多路径实时视频传输中的价值。

Abstract: Multipath transports place a client's WiFi, cellular, and satellite networks under one connection, yet real-time video gains little from them. The scheduler that assigns packets to paths sees only bytes, so it cannot tell a keyframe that anchors a second of video from an enhancement frame whose loss costs one image. We show that the limiting factor is not a shortage of path diversity but the absence of a channel through which the application can name what the transport cannot see. Media over QUIC Transport (MoQT), already deployed on production CDNs, carries media as named Objects, and relays forward each Object's metadata without interpreting it. The knowledge the scheduler lacks therefore already flows through the subscriber's relay. We present MoMQ, a MoQT extension that turns this metadata into path decisions. Applications and relay operators install declarative rules at the edge relay that match Object metadata and express delivery preferences against labeled paths. The relay evaluates the rules mechanically, so it acts on video semantics while containing no video logic. On a live testbed spanning Starlink and WiFi, four rules cut P99.9 frame completion time from 384.7 ms under the best transport-only scheduler to 114.1 ms and reduce the required playback buffer by 61.5%. MoMQ is the only configuration that meets the 150 ms interactive latency target. Across relays in two countries and subscribers on two continents, the same rules apply unchanged and retain their advantage wherever the paths remain disjoint.

</details>


### [217] [Energy-Efficient Joint Optimization of VLC and Fog Computing Resources Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10752)
*Wafaa B. M. Fadlelmula,Sanaa Hamid Mohamed,Taisir E. H. El-Gorashi,Jaafar M. H. Elmirghani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In this paper, we consider an indoor networking architecture in which visible light communication (VLC) provides wireless connectivity between users and distributed fog computing resources and an energy-efficient passive optical network (PON)- based backhaul interconnects the VLC access points (APs). We investigate the joint optimization of the allocation of VLC and fog computing resources aiming to minimize processing and networking power consumption. We develop a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model that jointly optimizes access points (APs) selection, wavelength assignment and fog computing resources allocation to serve the requests of users. Compared to a statically preconfigured AP selection and wavelength assignment based on maximizing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) independently of fog resource placement, the joint optimization achieves lower average total power consumption, driven by a reduction in processing power. These savings are attributed to the ability of the joint optimization to allow AP sharing across users and selecting more energy-efficient wavelengths that still satisfy quality of service (QoS) requirements.

</details>


### [218] [Multi-UAV Tracking Evaluation Using 5G Uplink Signals on an O-RAN ISAC Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10784)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究利用5G O-RAN系统重新利用上行探测参考信号作为被动雷达波形，评估多目标检测、关联和跟踪性能。发现目标检测后主要限制是目标竞争（74%的相干处理区间内两个目标共享一个最近检测），而非灵敏度；仰角无法分离范围-多普勒单元内的目标，但能决定58%区间内的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对反无人机指挥控制系统(C2)需求，评估多目标检测、关联和跟踪性能，而非仅关注检测能力。研究5G NR上行探测参考信号作为被动雷达波形的可行性。

Method: 基于OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT构建O-RAN仿真测试平台，采用8元面阵接收的 bistatic配置，使用三架不同高度、速度和雷达截面积(-8至-20 dBsm)的UAV。将RIC xApp并发轨迹通过SAPIENT接口导出到C2融合节点，携带经过校准的检测置信度。评估采用单一几何构型和10个噪声种子的纯仿真方式。

Result: 1) 一旦所有目标被检测，主要限制是竞争而非灵敏度；2) 约74%相干处理区间内两个目标共享一个最近检测；3) 目标被检测的频率远高于被跟踪的频率；4) 面阵仰角无法分离范围-多普勒单元内共享的目标；5) 仰角在58%区间内决定关联。

Conclusion: 研究通过表征和分析机制，识别了影响多目标跟踪性能的关键因素。结果表明，对于反无人机C2应用，在目标检测后，目标竞争(而非灵敏度)是主要瓶颈，且仰角有助于数据关联但无法解决相同范围-多普勒单元内的目标分离问题。

Abstract: We evaluate multi-target detection, association and tracking end to end on an O-RAN simulation testbed built from OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT that repurposes the 5G NR uplink sounding reference signal as a passive radar waveform, and against what a counter-UAS command-and- control (C2) consumer requires rather than by detection alone. Three UAVs differing in altitude, velocity and radar cross section (-8 to -20 dBsm) fly one bistatic pair with an 8-element planar receive array. Once every target is detected the binding limit is contention, not sensitivity - two targets share one nearest detection in about 74% of coherent processing intervals, and targets are detected far more often than they are tracked. Elevation from that array cannot separate targets sharing a range-Doppler cell, but it decides association in 58% of intervals. Concurrent tracks are exported from the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) xApp to a C2 fusion node over a SAPIENT interface carrying a calibrated detection confidence, validated at schema level against a mock fusion node. The evaluation is emulation-only on one geometry with 10 noise seeds, the mechanisms are characterized and analyzed to identify the key factors influencing multi-target tracking performance.

</details>


### [219] [A Systematic Sample Size Analysis of ML-Based Path Loss Prediction for LPWAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11083)
*Robert Bitterling,Christian Nettersheim,Jörn Hees,Michael Rademacher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 机器学习模型（尤其是使用激光雷达地形特征的随机森林）在城市LoRa网络的路径损耗预测中表现优于传统经验模型，训练集最大时RMSE低于6.5 dB（基线模型最优为9.7 dB），但对未见过的网关泛化能力存在差异：随机森林表现中等下降，而仅基于坐标的k近邻模型显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗广域网（如LoRa）在智慧城市场景中日益普及，有效网络规划需要准确的路径损耗预测；传统经验传播模型在此类场景下的精度有限，因此需要探索机器学习方法以提升预测性能。

Method: 基于城市真实测量数据，系统分析训练集规模对预测精度的影响。采用两种机器学习模型：(1) 使用激光雷达衍生地形特征的随机森林（RF）；(2) 使用坐标数据的k近邻（k-NN）。将其与已有经验模型及专用LPWAN模型进行对比，并采用随机池化划分和留一网关交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 在随机池化划分下，两种机器学习模型在所有训练集规模上均持续优于基线模型。训练集最大时，RMSE均低于6.5 dB，而最佳基线模型为9.7 dB，表明模型在部署内插值准确。留一网关验证显示：随机森林对未见到网关的泛化能力与位置相关，部分网关误差中等增加，部分误差较大；仅基于坐标的k-NN在遇到未见网关时性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法，特别是融合激光雷达地形特征的随机森林，可显著提升LoRa在城市部署中的路径损耗预测精度，实现良好的部署内插值，但对未见网关的泛化能力存在位置依赖性；仅依赖坐标的k-NN模型鲁棒性较差，未来需结合更多环境特征以增强跨网关泛化。

Abstract: Low Power Wide Area Networks like LoRa are increasingly deployed for smart city applications, requiring accurate path loss prediction for effective network planning. Traditional (empirical) propagation models often exhibit limited accuracy in these scenarios. We investigate machine learning models for LoRa path loss prediction, systematically analyzing how prediction accuracy scales with training set size using real-world measurements from an urban deployment. Our approach employs a Random Forest with LiDAR-derived terrain features and k-Nearest Neighbors with coordinate data, comparing their performance against established empirical models and specialized LPWAN models. Under random pooled splits, both ML models consistently outperform the considered baseline models across the evaluated training-set sizes. At maximum training size, they achieve RMSE values below 6.5 dB compared to 9.7 dB for the best baseline, indicating accurate within-deployment interpolation. A leave-one-gateway-out check qualifies this result: RF shows placement-dependent transfer to held-out gateways, with moderate degradation for several gateways but larger errors for others, whereas coordinate-only k-NN degrades substantially when the gateway location is unseen

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Pinching-Antenna Systems: From Antenna Placement to Antenna Roaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10136)
*Kaidi Wang,Daniel K. C. So,Zhiguo Ding*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对有限移动速度的针缩天线系统，提出天线漫游这一新型操作模式，并与传统天线放置进行统一周期比较。通过动态规划方法优化和率，仿真验证了天线漫游在定位开销较大时的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于天线移动速度有限，传统天线放置模式存在不可忽略的重定位延迟，导致信道质量与有效传输时间之间存在权衡，因此需要研究新的操作模式以克服这一限制。

Method: 提出天线漫游模式，即天线沿波导连续移动同时服务用户；建立统一周期持续时间的框架比较两种模式；解析推导和率差异；构建和率最大化问题；将连续优化问题转化为有限状态序列决策问题，利用基于动态规划的算法求解；针对天线漫游，解析刻画最优无约束服务区间划分。

Result: 解析结果表明天线漫游避免了专用定位开销，但代价是空间平均导致的信道质量下降；仿真验证了理论分析，展示了天线漫游相对于天线放置的性能优势，尤其是在定位开销较大时，并证实了基于动态规划方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 天线漫游在特定条件下可优于传统天线放置，特别是定位开销显著时；基于动态规划的优化方法能有效提升可达和率。

Abstract: This paper investigates pinching-antenna systems with finite antenna movement speed, under which conventional antenna placement is subject to non-negligible repositioning delay, resulting in a fundamental tradeoff between channel quality and effective transmission time. In this context, antenna roaming is proposed as a novel operation mode, in which the antenna moves continuously along the waveguide while simultaneously serving users. By incorporating communication and antenna movement into the same transmission cycle, a unified cycle-duration framework is established to facilitate a fair comparison between antenna placement and antenna roaming. The sum-rate difference is then analytically derived, indicating that antenna roaming avoids dedicated positioning overhead with a loss in channel quality due to spatial averaging. The corresponding sum rate maximization problems are formulated for the two operation modes. The continuous optimization problems are transformed into finite-state sequential decision problems and solved via dynamic programming (DP) based algorithms. For antenna roaming, the optimal unconstrained service-interval partition is analytically characterized, with each antenna position assigned to the user achieving the highest instantaneous rate. Simulation results validate the theoretical analysis, demonstrate the performance advantage of antenna roaming over antenna placement, especially when positioning overhead is significant, and confirm the effectiveness of the DP based solutions in improving the achievable sum rate.

</details>


### [221] [Transceiver Design for Cell-Free Unsourced Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10159)
*Mert Ozates,Mohammad Kazemi,Eduard Jorswieck,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低复杂度、能量高效的无源随机接入（URA）无小区方案，利用分布式接入点与集中处理单元的前传连接，用户发送导频序列和极化码字（按开关模式放置），采用迭代解码（基于OMP的导频检测与信道估计、线性MMSE符号估计、符号合并、单用户解码和 successive 干扰消除（SIC））。有限块长（FBL）分析和复杂度分析表明，所提方案性能优于现有低复杂度方案，最高提升4.5 dB，可支持1800个活跃用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无小区无源随机接入需要可扩展、低复杂度的解决方案，以处理海量用户接入，同时降低能耗和计算负担。

Method: 提出一种系统，用户发送导频和极化码字（开关模式）。接收端采用迭代解码：基于OMP的导频检测与信道估计、线性MMSE符号估计、符号合并、单用户解码和SIC。同时进行有限块长性能分析和详细复杂度分析。

Result: 数值结果表明，FBL分析准确刻画了系统性能。所提方案相比现有低复杂度方案性能提升最高达4.5 dB，可支持多达1800个活跃用户。

Conclusion: 所提无小区URA方案在性能和扩展性上优于现有低复杂度方案，经FBL分析和数值结果验证。

Abstract: We propose a low-complexity, energy-efficient solution for cell-free unsourced random access (URA), in which multiple distributed access points are connected to a central processing unit via a fronthaul. Assuming that each user transmits a pilot sequence followed by a polar codeword whose symbols are placed to the data part of the frame according to an on-off pattern, we utilize iterative decoding, including orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP)-based pilot detection and channel estimation, symbol estimation using a linear minimum mean square error (MMSE) solution, symbol combining, single-user decoding, and successive interference cancellation (SIC). We also present finite blocklength (FBL) performance and detailed complexity analyses. Numerical results demonstrate that the FBL analysis properly characterizes system performance and that the proposed cell-free URA scheme offers superior performance compared to existing low-complexity schemes. Namely, it offers a superior performance of up to 4.5 dB and can accommodate up to 1800 active users.

</details>


### [222] [A JEPA-Based Field-Layer World Model for Bridging Channel Prediction and Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10222)
*Yuzhi Yang,Brahim Mefgouda,Hang Zou,Lina Bariah,Anis Bara,Yuhuan Lu,Hao Zhang,MérouaneDebbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于JEPA的场层世界模型(FWM)，通过多分辨率CSI观测学习共享潜在传播状态并在潜在空间预测其演化，显著提升了MIMO-OFDM系统的符号检测和波束成形性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO-OFDM系统中CSI的直接系数级预测在真实传播环境下脆弱，空间扰动、局部散射变化和相位变化会导致复杂信道域中的大误差。然而，底层无线传播场仍包含跨越时间、频率、天线和载波维度的稳定可预测结构，这些结构未被充分利用。

Method: 提出基于JEPA的场层世界模型，通过尺度特定的分词器头将多分辨率CSI观测映射到共享的潜在传播场空间；训练潜在预测主干以推断掩码或未来场状态；采用增量多尺度对齐策略，允许在不从头重训的情况下纳入新观测尺度；下游重建时，将预测的潜在场作为结构化先验与稀疏当前导频结合。

Result: 单带和跨带重建实验显示符号检测性能提升，更重要的是波束成形增益显著，尽管NMSE改善有限；表明FWM捕获了超越系数级CSI拟合的与任务相关的空间传播结构。

Conclusion: 在传播场层面而非系数层面学习无线信道模型，能够有效利用传播场的稳定可预测结构，在保持较低计算成本的同时，实现显著的波束成形和符号检测性能提升，为MIMO-OFDM系统的CSI预测提供了更稳健的框架。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI) acquisition, reconstruction, and prediction are fundamental yet costly tasks in modern MIMO-OFDM wireless systems. Direct coefficient-level prediction of raw CSI is fragile in realistic propagation environments, since small spatial perturbations, local scattering changes, and phase variations can cause large errors in the complex channel domain. However, the underlying wireless propagation field still contains stable and predictable structures that can be exploited across time, frequency, antenna, and carrier dimensions. Motivated by this observation, we propose a JEPA-based field-layer world model (FWM) that learns a shared latent propagation state from multi-resolution CSI observations across the considered carrier bands and predicts its task-relevant evolution in the latent domain. The proposed FWM maps multiple CSI observation resolutions to a shared latent propagation-field space through scale-specific tokenizer heads. A latent prediction backbone is then trained to infer masked or future field states, while an incremental multi-scale alignment strategy allows new observation scales to be incorporated without retraining the entire model from scratch. For downstream reconstruction, the predicted latent field is used as a structured prior and combined with sparse current pilots. Experiments on single-band and cross-band reconstruction demonstrate improved symbol detection and, more notably, substantial beamforming gains despite modest NMSE improvements, indicating that FWM captures task-relevant spatial propagation structure beyond coefficient-wise CSI fitting.

</details>


### [223] [Achievable Accuracy and Cramer Rao Bounds for SSB Based LEO Positioning in NR NTN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10270)
*Rainer Bachl,Tingting Lei,Muhammad Nabeel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对Starlink等先进LEO卫星网络采用5G NR NTN标准后，利用Synchronization Signal Blocks (SSB) 进行机会性定位的理论极限问题，推导出单SSB的CRLB，并构建多时域、多卫星的Fisher信息框架，验证了在真实SNR下可达CRLB且实现亚米级定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LEO卫星网络如Starlink MSS的部署，5G NR NTN标准提供了全球覆盖的同步信号块，为接收机定位提供了机会。然而，SSB定位的理论性能极限尚未系统研究，需要量化延迟和载频观测量的估计精度，以指导系统设计。

Method: 作者基于完整SSB时频能量分布推导单SSB的CRLB，将延迟和载频观测量分别对应伪距和多普勒。随后扩展到多时域、多卫星的Fisher信息矩阵，联合约束用户位置、时钟偏置和时钟漂移。每个SSB的贡献按基于接收SNR的范围依赖CRLB加权，使估计器与界共享相同噪声模型。提出物理加权最小二乘估计器。

Result: 仿真显示所提出的CRLB与实际可达性能高度吻合，估计器在真实工作SNR下接近CRLB。以Starlink星座为例，分析地面用户接收SNR后，证明可实现亚米级定位精度。

Conclusion: 本文建立了SSB定位的理论极限，并验证了在LEO卫星网络中的可行性，为未来基于5G NR NTN的高精度定位系统提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Advanced Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks, such as Starlinks Mobile Satellite Service (MSS), will adopt the 5G New Radio (NR) Non-Terrestrial Network (NTN) standard. This enables the use of ubiquitous Synchronization Signal Blocks (SSBs) for opportunistic receiver positioning based on pseudorange and Doppler measurements. In this work, we characterize the estimation theoretic limits of SSB-based positioning by deriving single SSB Cramer Rao lower bounds (CRLBs) for delay and carrier frequency observables associated with pseudorange and Doppler. These bounds are obtained using the full SSB time-frequency energy distribution and extended into a multi epoch, multi satellite Fisher information framework that jointly bounds stationary user position, clock bias, and clock drift. Each SSB contribution is weighted according to a range dependent CRLB determined by the received SNR, so the estimator and the bound share a common noise model. In simulation, the resulting bound closely matches achievable performance, and a physically weighted least squares estimator approaches the CRLB at realistic operating SNR. Using a Starlink based constellation, we analyze the operating SNR experienced by a ground user and demonstrate sub-meter positioning accuracy.

</details>


### [224] [Neural implants and human safety: single-fault detection for DC-coupled recording front ends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10361)
*Dimitris Antoniadis,Timothy Constandinou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对直流耦合神经植入前端中因单点故障（如LNA输入管击穿）导致的脑组织DC电流泄露问题，提出了一种基于电流饥饿弛豫振荡器的故障检测机制，可在0.81ms内检测到6.4nA的最坏故障电流，符合ISO 14708-3标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流耦合模拟前端（AFEs）虽能为神经植入提供低面积解决方案，但去除耦合电容后丧失了保护皮层组织的内在屏障。当LNA输入晶体管发生栅氧化层击穿等单点故障时，会在供电轨与大脑之间形成直接DC通路，而记录端的此类安全隐患迄今几乎未被研究。

Method: 设计了一种单点故障检测机制，通过监测LNA因故障产生的直流不平衡来检测异常。该机制采用电流饥饿弛豫振荡器，将不平衡量编码为占空比，并以时间-数字转换方式进行读取。整个设计基于65nm工艺实现。

Result: 在65nm工艺下，该机制可在所有工艺角下于0.81ms内解析6.4nA的最坏故障，符合ISO 14708-3标准对8533 μm²电极的安全限值要求。

Conclusion: 所提故障检测机制有效解决了直流耦合神经记录前端的安全隐患问题，在满足国际安全标准的前提下，为直流耦合神经植入系统的安全设计提供了可行方案，开启了关于直流耦合记录安全性的更广泛讨论。

Abstract: DC-coupled analogue front ends (AFEs) for neural implants provide a low-area solution. However, removing the coupling capacitor eliminates the intrinsic barrier that protects cortical tissue: a single-fault event, such as gate-oxide breakdown of a low-noise amplifier (LNA) input transistor, can open a direct DC path from the supply rail into the brain. On the stimulation side this hazard is well understood, and single-fault tolerance is enforced by a series DC-blocking capacitor; on the recording side, DC-coupled front ends discard the equivalent safeguard, yet their protection has gone almost unexamined. This paper presents a single-fault detection mechanism that monitors the LNA for the DC imbalance produced by such a failure and disables the amplifier before the resulting fault current can irreversibly damage tissue. The imbalance is encoded in the duty cycle of a current-starved relaxation oscillator and read out as a time-to-digital measurement. Designed in 65 nm, the mechanism resolves a worst-case fault of 6.4 nA across all corners within 0.81 ms - compliant with the ISO~14708-3 limit for an 8533 um2 electrode - opening a broader discussion of safety in DC-coupled recording.

</details>


### [225] [Digital Twin-Aided Prescreening for User Scheduling in MU-MIMO Downlink Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10364)
*Namhyun Kim,Mahmoud Saad Abouamer,Jeonghun Park,Petar Popovski,Ahmed Alkhateeb*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 数字孪生用户预选框架(DiTUS)通过数字孪生技术在进行瞬时CSI获取前预选优质用户，在128个候选用户、64用户CSI预算条件下，DiTUS-L相比传统SUS方法吞吐量提升15.8%，有效保持多用户分集增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集部署的大规模MU-MIMO系统中，基站每个调度间隔只能获取有限用户子集的瞬时CSI，严重限制了多用户分集增益的获取。需要在有限CSI预算下高效识别最优用户组合，避免传统方法需要获取所有用户全维度CSI的高开销问题。

Method: 提出DiTUS数字孪生辅助预选框架：(1)基于数字孪生推断的出发角和路径功率构建空间协方差矩阵；(2)可选使用高斯过程校准缓解路径功率偏差；(3)采用DiTUS-P(投影能量规则)或DiTUS-L(贪婪对数行列式规则)进行预选；(4)两级协议收集标量波束报告并仅向调度集请求r维有效信道向量；(5)支持比例公平调度。

Result: 在15dB数字孪生误差条件下，128个候选用户、64用户有效CSI获取预算、64用户候选名单的仿真显示：DiTUS-L达到35.06±0.49 bps/Hz吞吐量，而采用64用户全维度CSI的传统半正交用户选择(SUS)方法仅为30.28±0.60 bps/Hz，性能提升约15.8%。

Conclusion: 数字孪生辅助的用户预选方法能够在获取有效信道向量之前识别出强且具有空间兼容性的用户，在有限的CSI获取预算下保持显著的多用户分集增益，为大规模MU-MIMO系统提供了高效的用户选择解决方案。

Abstract: In dense deployments, massive multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) base stations can acquire instantaneous channel state information (CSI) for only a limited subset of users per scheduling interval, restricting multiuser diversity. We therefore propose Digital Twin User pre-Screening (DiTUS), a digital-twin (DT)-aided framework that identifies promising users before instantaneous CSI acquisition. DiTUS forms spatial covariances from DT-inferred departure angles and path powers. Optional Gaussian-process (GP) calibration mitigates path-power bias, while the dominant rank-r eigenspace of the aggregate covariance yields common reference beams. It prescreens the pool using DiTUS-P, a low-complexity projection-energy rule, or DiTUS-L, a greedy log-determinant rule that promotes spatial compatibility. A two-level protocol collects scalar beam reports from shortlisted users and requests r-dimensional effective-channel vectors only from the scheduled set. The framework also supports proportional-fair scheduling. At 15 dB under DT imperfections, simulations with 128 candidates, a 64-user effective-CSI acquisition budget, and a 64-user shortlist show that DiTUS-L achieves 35.06 +/- 0.49 bps/Hz versus 30.28 +/- 0.60 bps/Hz for semi-orthogonal user selection (SUS) with full-dimensional CSI from 64 users, demonstrating that DT-based prescreening preserves substantial multiuser-diversity gains by identifying strong, spatially compatible users before acquiring effective-channel vectors.

</details>


### [226] [Covert Communication with Spatially Heterogeneous User Cooperation Against a Geometry-Aware Warden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10446)
*Hyeonsik Yeom,Jinyoung Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究隐蔽通信场景：单个隐蔽用户由多个空间分布的非隐蔽用户协助，对抗几何感知型守门人。与以往基于接收端激活、同质信道或平均几何的工作不同，本文保留了用户到守门人的大规模衰落系数，用于协作用户激活和守门人检测分析。通过大规模用户部署的分析模型，建立了平衡鲍勃端干扰和守门人端干扰有效性的开关激活结构，并推导了检测误差概率、最优检测门限及满足隐蔽约束所需最少协作用户数的闭式近似。将高维功率控制问题降为一维分段搜索，提出低复杂度算法。分析表明：守门人端空间分散度越大，所需协作用户越少；用户到守门人平均距离越大，所需协作用户越多。数值实验验证了理论分析，有限样本检测接近大样本基准，而不完美的用户到鲍勃信道估计主要通过激活误差降低可达速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐蔽通信中，如何通过多个非隐蔽用户协助隐蔽用户，在几何感知型守门人存在的情况下实现更高效的隐蔽传输，是一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。以往工作常采用接收端激活、同质信道或平均几何等简化假设，无法准确刻画真实场景中用户到守门人的空间分布及大规模衰落特性。因此，需要建立更贴合实际、考虑个体用户到守门人大规模衰落系数的分析框架，以优化协作用户激活与功率控制，提升隐蔽通信性能。

Method: 本文针对大规模用户部署场景，建立了一种基于个体用户到守门人大规模衰落系数的隐蔽通信分析模型。首先，设计了一种开关式协作用户激活结构，旨在平衡对合法接收端（Bob）造成的干扰和对守门人检测有效性产生的干扰。基于聚合干扰统计特性，推导了检测误差概率的闭式近似表达式、最小化检测误差的最优检测门限，以及满足隐蔽约束所需的最少协作用户数量。进一步，将原本高维的功率控制优化问题转化为低维的分段搜索问题，并开发了一种避免依赖于分辨率的网格搜索的低复杂度算法。最后，通过数值仿真实验验证理论分析的正确性，并评估有限样本检测性能及信道估计误差对系统的影响。

Result: 理论分析表明，守门人侧的空间分散度增加可以有效降低所需协作用户的数量，而用户到守门人平均距离增大则会增加所需协作用户数量。数值结果证实了分析的正确性：有限样本下的检测性能趋近于大样本基准，说明所提出的近似方法具有良好的一致性。此外，当用户到Bob的信道估计存在误差时，主要通过引起激活错误而导致可达速率下降，但隐蔽性约束仍然可以满足。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种考虑几何感知守门人与空间分布协作用户的隐蔽通信新框架。通过保留个体用户到守门人大规模衰落系数，建立了更精确的干扰与检测模型，实现了协作用户激活与功率控制的联合优化。研究揭示了空间分散度与距离对隐蔽通信需求的影响规律，并给出了低复杂度的实现算法。结果表明，该框架能够在有限用户场景下逼近大样本理论极限，且对信道估计误差具有鲁棒性，为实际隐蔽通信系统的设计提供了有价值的理论指导和工程参考。

Abstract: This paper investigates covert communication in which a single covert user is assisted by multiple spatially distributed non-covert users against a geometry-aware warden. Unlike prior works based on receiver-centric activation, homogeneous channels, or averaged geometry, the proposed framework retains the individual user-to-warden large-scale fading coefficients in both cooperative user activation and the warden's detection analysis. Under the adopted analytical formulation for large-scale user deployments, we establish an on--off activation structure that balances the interference caused at Bob and the interference effectiveness at warden. Based on the resulting aggregate-interference statistics, we derive a closed-form approximation of the detection error probability, the detection threshold that minimizes it, and the minimum number of cooperative users required to satisfy the covert constraint. Then, we reduce the original high-dimensional power-control problem to a one-dimensional piecewise search and develop a low-complexity algorithm that avoids resolution-dependent grid search. The analysis shows that greater warden-side spatial dispersion can reduce the required cooperative users, whereas a larger average user-to-warden distance increases it. Numerical results validate the analysis and show that finite-sample detection approaches the large-sample benchmark, while imperfect user-to-Bob channel estimation mainly reduces the achievable rate through activation errors.

</details>


### [227] [AI-Assisted ISAC Localization-as-a-Service for 6G UAV-IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10571)
*Ruhul Amin Khalil*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出AIRS-LaaS，一种AI辅助的ISAC资源选择框架，通过轻量化选择器激活少量锚点-波束对，实现6G UAV-IoT网络中的定位服务，在定位精度、通信效率和资源开销之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G UAV-IoT网络需要可靠的位置即服务（LaaS），但传统全锚点/波束激活导致导频开销、能耗和波束训练延迟增加。需要智能资源选择机制来优化性能与效率。

Method: 提出AIRS-LaaS，一个边缘智能框架，基于视距（LoS）可能性、信干噪比（SINR）、感知置信度、几何、移动风险和资源成本对候选锚点-波束对进行排序，并使用轻量化选择器激活一个紧凑子集。

Result: 仿真比较了AIRS-LaaS与全锚点、FIM/CRLB贪婪、最强SINR、最近锚点和随机方案在LoS/NLoS条件和UAV移动性下的性能，结果显示AIRS-LaaS在定位精度、通信效率和资源开销之间实现了良好平衡。

Conclusion: AIRS-LaaS为6G UAV-IoT网络中的LaaS提供了有效的资源选择方案，并讨论了标准驱动的关键性能指标（KPIs）、ISAC报告、定位置信度、回退操作、AI模型管理和隐私感知数据交换等关键问题。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) can enable sixth-generation (6G) unmanned aerial vehicle-assisted Internet of Things (UAV-IoT) networks to provide reliable Localization-as-a-Service (LaaS), but activating all aerial/terrestrial anchors and beams increases pilot overhead, energy use, and beam-training delay. This article proposes artificial intelligence (AI)-assisted ISAC resource selection for LaaS (AIRS-LaaS), an edge-intelligent framework that ranks candidate anchor--beam pairs using line-of-sight (LoS) likelihood, signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR), sensing confidence, geometry, mobility risk, and resource cost. A lightweight selector then activates only a compact subset before localization. Simulations compare AIRS-LaaS with all-anchor, Fisher information matrix/Cramér--Rao lower bound (FIM/CRLB)-greedy, strongest-SINR, nearest-anchor, and random schemes under LoS/non-line-of-sight (NLoS) conditions and UAV mobility. Results show a balanced localization--communication--overhead tradeoff, while the discussion highlights standard-driven key performance indicators (KPIs), ISAC reporting, localization confidence, fallback operation, AI model management, and privacy-aware data exchange.

</details>


### [228] [Statistical Channel Model for FSO Systems Assisted by a UAV-Mounted IRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10603)
*Ferdaous Tarhouni,Vasilis K. Papanikolaou,Laura Cottatellucci,Mohammad-Ali Khalighi,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出将光学智能反射面（IRS）集成到无人机（UAV）上，以放宽自由空间光（FSO）系统的视距约束，扩大覆盖范围，并增强部署灵活性。针对UAV悬停导致的位置和方向偏移误差，本文基于惠更斯-菲涅尔原理，推导出适用于任意入射和反射传播方向的入射和反射电场表达式。在此基础上，建立了一个闭环统计信道模型，考虑了几何损耗和不对准损耗，并假设UAV位置和方向服从高斯波动，对接收镜头的光束不对准进行了统计建模。通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证了模型的准确性，并给出了最小化中断概率的UAV挂载IRS最优部署设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FSO系统受视距限制且部署灵活性低，难以适应复杂环境。利用IRS可反射光束实现非视距通信，但UAV平台引入的抖动会导致严重不对准误差，降低链路质量。因此，需要针对UAV-IRS-FSO链路建立准确的信道模型，以指导系统设计与优化。

Method: 基于惠更斯-菲涅尔原理，推导了光学IRS上任意入射和反射方向的电场表达式；结合几何与不对准损耗，构建统计信道模型；假设UAV位置和姿态波动为高斯分布，推导接收端光束不对准的统计模型；通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证模型。

Result: 得到闭环统计信道模型，能准确预测在随机UAV波动下的信道特性；仿真验证表明理论模型与MC结果一致；基于模型提出了UAV-IRS最优部署策略，可最小化中断概率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的理论框架为UAV辅助IRS的FSO系统设计提供了可靠的分析工具，有助于在实际应用中克服不对准问题，提升系统性能。

Abstract: Integrating optical intelligent reflecting surfaces (IRSs) into aerial platforms, such as unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), has been proposed to relax the line-of-sight (LoS) constraint, extend coverage, and enhance deployment flexibility of free space optical (FSO) systems. However, misalignment errors induced by the UAV hovering, in both position and orientation, may degrade connectivity and impair the end-to-end channel quality. In this paper, we develop novel expressions for the electric fields incident on and reflected by an optical IRS, based on the Huygens-Fresnel principle. The resulting expressions are applicable for any combination of incident and reflected propagation directions. Building on this framework, we derive a closed-form statistical channel model that captures the geometric and misalignment losses of an FSO link assisted by a UAV-mounted IRS in the presence of random UAV fluctuations. In particular, we develop a statistical model for the beam misalignment at the receiver lens, assuming Gaussian fluctuations in both the UAV position and orientation. The proposed analytical model is validated through Monte Carlo (MC) simulations and is further used to provide practical design guidelines regarding the optimal placement of the UAV-mounted IRS for the minimization of the outage probability.

</details>


### [229] [Robust Beamforming and Power Allocation for Coherent Cell-Free Massive MIMO with Residual Calibration Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10637)
*Mingjun Sun,Xidong Mu,Shaochuan Wu,Chongjun Ouyang,Hyundong Shin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究时分双工cell-free大规模MIMO系统中考虑校准老化的鲁棒下行传输方法，提出时变残余校准误差模型，并设计基于此模型的鲁棒波束成形和功率分配方案，通过加权最小均方误差优化框架实现高效求解，仿真验证了算法收敛性、鲁棒性能提升及性能-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将残余校准误差视为静态损伤，无法准确反映校准老化过程对系统性能的动态影响。本文旨在建立更精确的时变残余校准误差模型，并基于此模型设计能够应对校准老化效应的鲁棒传输方案，以提升cell-free大规模MIMO系统的实际部署性能。

Method: 1. 建立时变残余校准误差模型，刻画残余相位失配、残余载波频偏和振荡器相位噪声的联合效应；2. 考虑两种处理架构：即时校准信道鲁棒波束成形和统计波束成形信道鲁棒功率分配；3. 推导可达速率下界，明确校准老化对相干合并、波束成形增益不确定性和用户间干扰的影响；4. 以可达速率下界为设计指标，构建数据传输间隔内的加权速率和最大化问题；5. 提出基于高斯-勒让德积分的加权最小均方误差优化框架，在每AP功率约束下以封闭形式实现AP块方式的鲁棒波束成形和功率分配更新。

Result: 仿真结果表明：(i) 所提算法表现出稳定的收敛性；(ii) 考虑校准老化的鲁棒波束成形和功率分配方案比非鲁棒方案获得更高的加权速率和；(iii) 鲁棒波束成形在频谱效率方面表现更优，而鲁棒功率分配在频谱效率与计算复杂度、前传开销等实现成本之间提供了更有利的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文针对cell-free大规模MIMO系统中的校准老化问题，建立了时变残余校准误差模型，并在此基础上设计了两种鲁棒传输架构。所提出的基于高斯-勒让德积分的WMMSE优化框架能够有效求解加权速率和最大化问题，在保持算法稳定收敛的同时，显著提升了系统性能。研究表明，鲁棒波束成形和鲁棒功率分配各有优势，可根据实际部署需求在频谱效率与实现成本之间进行灵活选择，为cell-free大规模MIMO系统的实用化提供了重要的技术参考。

Abstract: This paper investigates robust downlink transmission to tolerate calibration aging in time-division duplex cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (CF-mMIMO) systems with residual calibration errors (RCEs). Unlike existing studies that typically treat RCEs as static impairments, we develop a time-evolving RCE model that characterizes the joint effects of residual phase mismatches, residual carrier frequency offsets, and oscillator phase noise. Based on this model, two practical processing architectures are considered: instantaneous calibrated-channel-based robust beamforming (BF) and statistical beamformed-channel-based robust power allocation (PA). For both architectures, tractable achievable rate lower bounds are derived, which explicitly reveal the impact of calibration aging on coherent combining, BF-gain uncertainty, and inter-user interference. Using these lower bounds as design metrics, we formulate an effective weighted sum-rate (EWSR) maximization problem over the data transmission interval, so that the resulting BF and PA designs can account for the temporal evolution of RCEs rather than a single calibrated instant. To efficiently solve the resulting problems, a Gauss--Legendre quadrature-based weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) optimization framework is developed, where both robust BF and PA are updated in an access point (AP)-block manner with closed-form solutions under per-AP power constraints. Simulation results demonstrate that: i) the proposed algorithms exhibit stable convergence; ii) the proposed robust BF and PA schemes achieve higher EWSR by explicitly accounting for calibration aging than their non-robust counterparts; and iii) robust BF achieves higher spectral efficiency (SE), whereas robust PA provides a more favorable tradeoff between SE and implementation cost in terms of computational complexity and fronthaul overhead.

</details>


### [230] [Unified Analytical Framework for Emergency RIS-UAV Networks Under Practical Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10653)
*Yinong Chen,Wenchi Cheng,Jingqing Wang,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出ζ-Model统一框架，用于分析异构UAV-RIS网络在过时/不完美CSI和离散相位偏移等实际损伤条件下的应急无线通信性能，统一了信息论模型和工程基线模型，推导出平均容量、有效容量和中断概率的闭式表达式，经蒙特卡洛仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构UAV网络嵌入可重构智能表面(RIS)为恶劣环境下的应急无线通信(EWC)提供了增强覆盖和抗逆性的前景。然而，灾区极端条件要求在过时/不完美信道状态信息(CSI)和离散RIS相位偏移等实际损伤下进行鲁棒性能评估。现有工作缺乏统一的CSI误差建模分析框架，采用不一致的方法将误差视为信道增益或等效干扰，导致基准模糊不清。

Method: 提出ζ-Model，一种统一的接收机等效信噪比框架，通过参数ζ连续表征残余误差可利用性。该框架将信息论模型(ITM)和工程基线模型(EBM)分别作为乐观和悲观基准接收机处理进行统一，同时将简化工程模型(SEM)纳入作为可处理近似。采用Fisher-Snedecor F分布捕获严重衰落和阴影效应，基于矩匹配方法推导表达式。

Result: 在所提统一框架及其边界情况下，推导出平均容量(AC)、有效容量(EC)和中断概率(OP)的矩匹配闭式或有限和近似表达式及渐近表达式。通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证了推导结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架量化了性能极限，并为在各种信道条件和系统损伤下设计鲁棒高效的应急无线通信系统提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Heterogeneous unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) networks embedded with reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) present a promising paradigm for emergency wireless communications (EWC), offering enhanced coverage and resilience in harsh environments. However, extreme conditions in disaster areas necessitate robust performance evaluation under practical impairments, including outdated/imperfect channel state information (CSI) and discrete RIS phase shifts. Existing works lack a unified analytical framework for modeling CSI errors, employing inconsistent approaches that treat errors either as channel gain or as equivalent interference, leading to ambiguous benchmarks. To address this, we propose the $ζ$-Model, a unified receiver-equivalent signal-to-noise (SNR) framework that continuously parameterizes residual-error exploitability via $ζ$. This framework unifies the information-theoretic model (ITM) and the engineering baseline model (EBM) as the optimistic and pessimistic benchmark receiver treatments, while incorporating the simplified engineering model (SEM) as a tractable approximation. By employing the Fisher-Snedecor $\mathcal{F}$ distribution to capture severe fading and shadowing, we derive moment-matching-based closed-form or finite-sum approximate expressions and asymptotic expressions for average capacity (AC), effective capacity (EC), and outage probability (OP) under the proposed unified framework and its boundary cases. Validated by Monte Carlo simulations, our framework quantifies performance limits and provides crucial insights for designing robust and efficient EWC systems under various channel conditions and system impairments.

</details>


### [231] [Optimal Measurement Point Selection for Radio Environment Maps: A Two-Stage Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10686)
*Bastian Perner,Friedemann Laue,Maximilian Lubke,Norman Franchi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段测量选择框架，通过最优实验设计(OED)预选择减少候选集，再利用组合优化最小化平均绝对误差(MAE)来提高无线环境地图(REM)构建精度，实验验证显示可将测量位置数量减少同时提升精度，MAE降低幅度达4%-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁态势感知对于本地和临时非公共网络(NPN)的部署运行至关重要，无线环境地图(REM)为网络规划提供基础，但在时间和成本受限条件下，如何高效选择测量位置构建REM仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段测量选择框架：第一阶段基于最优实验设计(OED)策略生成候选集的预选择阶段；第二阶段采用组合优化方法，通过场景特定的传播仿真模型，选择优化后的测量位置子集以最小化REM的平均绝对误差(MAE)。

Result: 结果表明，所提框架在所有评估策略中均能减少所需测量位置数量同时持续改善REM构建精度。使用真实世界测量数据验证了方法的实用性，优化阶段实现MAE从约4%到约50%的降低，且不依赖于所选的预选择策略。

Conclusion: 该两阶段框架有效解决了REM构建中测量位置选择的问题，在严格时间和成本约束下，通过OED预选择与组合优化的结合，显著提升了REM构建效率与精度，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) situational awareness is essential for the deployment and operation of local and temporary non-public networks (NPNs). Radio Environment Maps (REMs) provide an effective basis for network planning and operation by capturing the prevailing EM conditions. However, efficient measurement location selection for REM construction remains a key challenge, particularly under strict time and cost constraints. This paper presents a two-stage measurement selection framework that employs Optimal Experimental Design (OED)-based strategies to generate a reduced candidate set during a pre-selection phase. In a subsequent optimization stage, combinatorial optimization is applied to select an improved subset of measurement locations by minimizing the Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of the resulting REM, using a scenario-specific propagation simulation model. The results demonstrate that the proposed framework reduces the number of required measurement locations while consistently improving REM construction accuracy across all evaluated strategies. Validation using real-world measurement data confirms the practical applicability of the approach, with the optimization stage achieving MAE reductions from about 4% to around 50%, independent of the chosen pre-selection strategy.

</details>


### [232] [Adaptive Source-Channel Coding for Bi-static Integrated Sensing and Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10898)
*Haotian Wang,Dan Wang,Xiaodong Xu,Chuan Huang,Hao Chen,Nan Ma,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于双静态集成感知与语义通信（ISSC）系统的感知感知自适应源-信道编码（SA-ASCC）和波束成形设计框架，通过联合优化语义通信任务的编码速率和感知任务的发射波束成形，实现了端到端语义失真最小化，仿真结果表明该方案优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信（SemCom）作为6G中集成感知与通信系统的新范式，能够通过传输任务相关的语义特征而非原始比特来提高传输效率。然而，现有工作主要集中在感知数据压缩以减少通信开销，未考虑语义通信与感知任务的集成传输框架，存在研究空白。

Method: 1. 通过推导包含源编码和信道编码组件的上界来近似端到端语义失真函数；2. 针对双静态ISSC系统中收发机部署在不同位置导致的不完美时间同步问题，推导目标位置的混合克拉美-罗界（HCRB）；3. 考虑HCRB阈值、信道使用次数和功率预算，建立失真最小化问题（非凸、混合整数规划）；4. 提出交替优化（AO）算法将问题分解为模型选择和联合速率及波束成形优化子问题；5. 分别采用穷举搜索和连续凸逼近与分数规划组合方法求解各子问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案在性能上优于DJSCC-WF-ZF和BPG-WF-ZF基准方法，验证了SA-ASCC框架在平衡语义通信与感知性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了集成感知与语义通信系统传输框架的研究空白，通过联合优化编码速率与波束成形，实现了语义失真与感知精度的有效平衡，为6G ISSC系统提供了理论支撑和实用化解决方案，展现了语义通信在提升系统整体性能方面的显著潜力。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) has emerged as a new paradigm to facilitate the performance of integrated sensing and communication systems in 6G, due to its potential to enhance transmission efficiency by transmitting task-relevant semantic features rather than raw bits. However, most of the existing works mainly focus on sensing data compression to reduce the subsequent communication overheads, without considering the integrated transmission framework for both the SemCom and sensing tasks. This paper proposes a sensing-aware adaptive source-channel coding (SA-ASCC) and beamforming design framework for bi-static integrated sensing and SemCom (ISSC) systems by jointly optimizing the coding rate for SemCom task and the transmit beamforming for both the SemCom and sensing tasks. Specifically, an end-to-end semantic distortion function is approximated by deriving an upper bound composing of source and channel coding induced components, and then a hybrid Cramér-Rao bound (HCRB) is derived for target position under imperfect time synchronization due to the transceiver deployed at different places in our considered bi-static ISSC system. To characterize the achievable region between SemCom and sensing performance, a distortion minimization problem is formulated by considering the HCRB threshold, channel uses, and power budget, which is non-convex due to the coupled design variables and the mixed-integer program. Subsequently, an alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is proposed to decompose this problem into the model selection and joint rate and beamforming optimization subproblems, which are solved by the exhaustive search method and the combination of successive convex approximation and fractional programming, respectively. Finally, simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme outperforms the DJSCC-WF-ZF and BPG-WF-ZF benchmarks.

</details>


### [233] [CII: Novel CSI-RS Metric for Joint Precoder and RIS Reporting in Multi-User NextG Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10901)
*Ali Fuat Sahin,Sefa Kayraklik,Ali Gorcin,Ibrahim Hokelek,Ertugrul Basar,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信道信息指示器(CII)的统一反馈框架，通过单一反馈指标联合表示基站预编码和RIS配置，显著降低信令开销同时提升系统性能，为下一代网络中的RIS集成提供了标准化解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构智能表面(RIS)是下一代(NextG)无线网络的关键使能技术之一，但由于关联的信令开销，联合基站(BS)预编码和被动RIS配置的高效反馈报告仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: 通过扩展标准兼容的信道状态信息参考信号(CSI-RS)框架，本文引入了一种新颖的信道信息指示器(CII)，在单用户多输入单输出(MISO)系统的单一反馈指标中联合表示活动BS预编码矩阵和被动RIS配置。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的统一反馈框架与传统的分离报告方案相比，显著减少了上行信令开销。此外，尽管反馈负载仅略有增加，但基于CII的方案在系统性能方面优于传统的预编码矩阵指示器(PMI)方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为NextG无线网络中的RIS集成提供了一种实用且兼容标准的解决方案。

Abstract: While reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) are among the key enablers for next-generation (NextG) wireless networks, efficient feedback reporting for joint base station (BS) precoding and passive RIS configuration remains a major challenge due to the associated signaling overhead. By extending the standard-compliant channel state information reference signal framework, this paper introduces a novel channel information indicator (CII) that jointly represents the active BS precoding matrix and passive RIS configuration within a single feedback metric for multi-user multiple-input single-output systems. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed unified feedback framework significantly reduces uplink signaling overhead compared with conventional disjoint reporting schemes. Furthermore, despite only a modest increase in the feedback payload, the proposed CII-based scheme outperforms conventional precoding matrix indicator approaches in terms of system performance, offering a practical and standards-compatible solution for RIS integration in NextG wireless networks.

</details>


### [234] [Laser-Diode LiFi With Diffused-Beam Optics: System-Level Modeling and a Cross-Validated ns-3 Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10950)
*Hussain Ahmad,Syed Muhammad Talha Gillani,Toheed Omer,Saleem Aslam*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于激光二极管的LiFi系统完整可复现模型，通过ns-3和Python模拟器交叉验证，量化了系统级性能，填补了硬件演示与系统级性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光二极管相比LED在室内光无线接入中具有一个数量级的带宽优势，但现有原型研究往往未能量化硬件演示与系统级性能之间的差距。

Method: 开发了一套完整的扩散光束LD LiFi收发机系统模型（500 mW激光源、全息扩散器整形光束、IM/DD接收机、自适应M-QAM调制），并将其嵌入两个交叉验证的模拟器中：开源ns-3模块提供全栈网络模拟（信道、物理层、ARQ MAC、网络设备等），以及用于蒙特卡洛验证的Python链路级引擎。

Result: 从硬件原型出发，识别并弥补了典型原型报告的不足：串行接口吞吐量上限被误认为光链路容量、噪声建模缺失、错误平台不可测、未考察的波束宽度/覆盖权衡。框架显示，同一光前端在消除2-Mbaud UART瓶颈并以250-MHz电带宽驱动时，在14 m处支持930 Mb/s净速率（16-QAM，HD-FEC阈值3.8e-3），在5 m处可扩展至1.86 Gb/s（256-QAM），OOK可维持至23.3 m；20°扩散器在桌面高度覆盖半径4.2 m的室内单元。ns-3网络模拟显示，在70%负载下饱和吞吐量接近物理层速率的93%，99百分位延迟低于0.11 ms。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个可复现的系统模型和模拟器，量化了LiFi系统性能，填补了原型研究的空白，并公开了所有模型、代码和图表。

Abstract: Laser diodes (LDs) promise an order-of-magnitude bandwidth advantage over light-emitting diodes for indoor optical wireless access, but reported prototype studies frequently leave the gap between hardware demonstrations and system-level performance unquantified. This paper develops a complete, reproducible system model of a diffused-beam LD LiFi transceiver - a 500-mW laser source beam-shaped by a holographic diffuser, an intensity-modulation/ direct-detection (IM/DD) receiver, and adaptive M-QAM signaling - and embeds it in two cross validated simulators: an open ns-3 module providing full-stack network simulation (channel, PHY, ARQ MAC, Net Device, IP/UDP/TCP) and a Python link-level engine used for Monte Carlo validation of all analytical error models. Starting from a hardware prototype that transferred data, real-time voice, and images over a 14-m line-of-sight link, we identify and close the technical gaps typical of prototype-class reports: serial-interface throughput ceilings misread as optical-link capacity, absent noise modeling, unmeasurable error floors, and unexamined beamwidth/coverage trade-offs. The framework shows that the same optical front end, freed of its 2-Mbaud UART bottleneck and driven at its 250-MHz electrical bandwidth, supports 930 Mb/s net at 14 m under a $3.8 \times 10^{-3}$ HD-FEC threshold with 16-QAM, scales to 1.86 Gb/s at 5 m with 256-QAM, and sustains on-off keying to 23.3 m; a $20^\circ$ diffuser covers a 4.2-m-radius cell of a standard room at desk height. Network simulations over the ns-3 stack yield saturation goodput within 7% of the PHY line rate and sub-0.11-ms 99th-percentile latency at 70% load. All models, code, and figures are released for reproduction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现，大语言模型在复杂生物系统中可从数据分析进化到自主AI指导实验，通过49通道植物传感器网络实现实时自然语言解释，并构建闭环系统优化微气候、表型协议和胁迫场景，验证显示在最小时间模式下生产周期缩短35%，能源优化模式能耗降低18%，且自主开发出暗诱导叶绿素积累策略实现67.9%节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估大语言模型在复杂生物系统中的应用潜力，推动从人工干预分析到自主AI控制转变，提高农业生产的成本效益并降低计算和专家劳动力需求。

Method: 构建基于49通道植物传感器网络（包括多光谱、电化学和介电模态）的数据驱动框架，利用LLM处理生物物理数据，实时评估植物生理并触发硬件执行器优化微气候、执行表型协议或诱导受控胁迫，通过三个案例研究验证：垂直农场、单株设置及生产规模部署。

Result: 验证显示：LLM自主调节全光谱、450nm和660nm照明，最小时间模式下生产周期缩短35%；能源优化模式能耗降低18%且栽培时间仅微增；自主开发暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，节能67.9%。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM转化为数字农业的自主副驾驶，提升成本效益，降低计算和专家劳动力约束，实现AI与生物学的直接接口，推动复杂生物系统和生态的数据驱动探索。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [236] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOT（采样策略观察树）框架，通过基于采样的树结构方法解释深度强化学习策略。SPOT是模型无关的，能够构建有限视野的解释树，展示策略的动作偏好及其下游演化。在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域的案例研究表明，该方法比单时间步特征归因方法更能揭示策略行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习智能体在复杂环境中表现出色，但其决策过程难以解释。现有的解释方法往往局限于单时间步的特征归因，无法展示策略的长期行为模式和下游演化。需要一种能够捕捉策略时间动态和可能未来轨迹的解释框架。

Method: SPOT框架通过采样动作并递归模拟后续状态，构建可解释的有限视野树结构。该方法需要策略和环境模拟器的访问权限。树结构提供策略动作偏好的经验表示及其可能下游演化。SPOT是模型无关的，适用于任何深度强化学习策略。

Result: 理论层面：提供了形式化保证，证明SPOT能够渐近恢复策略的唯一最可能动作，并刻画了在高熵策略下的不一致行为。实证层面：在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域进行案例研究，展示了树状表示如何用于检查策略偏好、比较替代未来轨迹，并揭示单时间步特征归因方法无法发现的下游行为。

Conclusion: SPOT框架提供了一种新颖的、基于采样的深度强化学习策略解释方法，通过有限视野树结构揭示策略的时间动态和决策模式。与单时间步特征归因方法相比，SPOT能够更好地展示策略的长期行为和可能的未来演化路径，为理解复杂强化学习系统提供了更有价值的解释工具。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [237] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [238] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了Green AI领域的研究，并通过实验评估6种深度学习模型的碳排放情况，发现训练阶段是主要排放来源，且模型复杂度的提升并不必然带来相应的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML技术广泛应用，其高能耗和碳排放问题日益受到关注。特别是大规模深度学习模型的部署，虽然提供了强大的预测能力，但需要大量计算资源，带来显著的环境影响。

Method: 1) 系统综述Green AI、Green DL及相关优化技术的研究现状；2) 评估比较多种碳排放测量工具；3) 在CPU环境下构建实验平台，实现6种深度学习模型完成多标签分类任务，量化并比较各模型的碳排放足迹。

Result: 1) 训练阶段是深度学习生命周期中碳排放的主要贡献者；2) 架构复杂度的增加并未系统性地转化为与之成正比的精度提升；3) 不同深度学习模型在碳足迹方面存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在模型选择和AI系统设计过程中整合可持续性考量的必要性，需要谨慎平衡预测性能与环境成本之间的关系。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [239] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCBM（不确定性门控关系推理框架），用于增强概念瓶颈模型在不稳定概念状态下的鲁棒推理能力，通过建模概念间关系并结合不确定性指导，提升解释性和预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型虽然提供了基于人类可理解概念的框架，但在概念不可靠（如缺失或错误）时缺乏鲁棒推理机制。误导性的语义证据可能通过瓶颈传播，影响解释质量和下游预测准确性，亟需解决这一关键问题。

Method: ReCBM在瓶颈中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排除），利用不确定性指导关系精炼，建模概念间证据交换方式，并通过不确定性调节每个概念在推理过程中的贡献度，形成不确定性门控的关系推理机制。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明：ReCBM在概念缺失和翻转场景下显著提升了概念恢复和任务恢复性能；支持不确定性感知干预；能够提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集，且不会降低下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ReCBM通过整合概念关系建模与不确定性门控机制，有效解决了概念瓶颈模型在不可靠概念状态下的鲁棒性问题，实现了更可靠的语义推理、解释生成和预测输出，为可解释AI提供了重要的方法论贡献。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>


### [240] [Automating and Scaling Behavioral Scientific Research on AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10030)
*Soo Yong Lee,Jongha Lee,Jaewan Chun,Hyunjin Hwang,Fanchen Bu,Ziv Ben-Zion,Taekwan Kim,Denny Borsboom,Jaemin Yoo,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AEROBAT，首个自动化AI代理行为科学研究的系统，通过自动生成假设、设计实验、执行模拟、分析结果和撰写报告的完整流程，实现了对12种目标行为79个假设的自动化研究，为26个假设提供了中等到强的统计证据，证明了自动化研究可补充和扩展手动研究的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在复杂环境中部署日益增多，理解其行为变得至关重要。然而，目前针对AI代理的行为科学研究仍依赖手工操作，耗时长、劳动强度大，需要自动化解决方案来提高研究效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出AEROBAT系统——首个多智能体系统，可自动化执行AI代理行为科学研究的完整流程：根据用户给定的任意目标行为，自动生成相关假设，设计并执行控制实验，进行行为评估，分析实验结果，并撰写研究报告。

Result: 对12种目标行为应用AEROBAT系统，共生成并测试79个假设，设计1,240个控制实验，执行23,512轮模拟。其中26个假设获得中等到强的统计证据支持，包括一些新颖假设。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明，AI代理行为科学的自动化研究能够有效地补充并扩展传统手动研究的范围和效率，为复杂AI代理行为理解提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [241] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)
*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHORUS 是一种用于硬件验证代码生成的后训练框架。它通过分阶段监督微调（SFT）和密集奖励强化学习（RL）创建多样化的专家模型，并通过模型合并或进一步后训练整合这些专家，最终在一个 4B 模型上取得了 88.0% 的 Pass@1 成绩，显著优于 671B 的 DeepSeek-R1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成方面取得了进展，但仅靠文本模仿不如可执行反馈可靠。硬件验证是现代芯片设计的重要环节，其中高覆盖率测试台刺激生成是关键任务。现有的标准 SFT 到 RL 流程存在性能瓶颈，需要更高效的框架来提升性能。

Method: 提出了 CHORUS 后训练框架。基于两个观察：首先，分阶段 SFT 可产生行为多样的检查点，密集奖励 RL 将其转化为具有不同任务级优势的专家；其次，通过无训练模型合并或进一步后训练可利用这些互补优势，从而超越单个最佳专家的性能。

Result: 将产生的专家整合到单个 4B 模型中，在 CVDP-ECov 基准测试上实现了 88.0% 的 Pass@1 准确率，比 671B 的 DeepSeek-R1 高出 13.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: CHORUS 通过整合行为多样的专家模型突破了传统 SFT 到 RL 流程的性能限制，证明了通过创建和利用这些专家，可以在硬件验证代码生成任务上实现优于现有大规模模型的效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [242] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MESA框架，通过动态选择并融合多种结构化的记忆视图，解决长程智能体在查询时从历史轨迹中提取证据的挑战。MESA在AMA-Bench上比最强基线提升8.5%，同时减少41%的证据token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体的轨迹包含数百步交错的推理、动作和观察，回答问题可能依赖历史深处埋藏的证据。外部记忆以结构化表示存储轨迹，但每种结构仅提供不完整视角。现有系统要么为每个查询读取固定结构集合（增加上下文噪声），要么将查询路由到单一结构（阻碍互补证据的组合）。实验分析表明最优记忆配置通常既非单一结构也非全结构联合，而是根据查询和任务需求量身定制的多结构组合。

Method: 提出结构级动态选择方法，从专门记忆结构库中选择并融合查询自适应子集。构建MESA框架，为每条轨迹建立五个互补的结构视图，通过端到端答案级反馈学习选择和融合查询特定子集，并采用基于先验引导的搜索和UCB引导调度的harness优化方法，在弱监督下平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在AMA-Bench基准测试中，MESA比最强基线性能提升8.5%，相比使用全部结构的替代方案减少41%的证据token消耗。

Conclusion: 动态选择并融合多种记忆结构的策略显著优于固定结构集合或单一结构方案，实现了更高精度和更高效率的长程智能体记忆检索。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [243] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，将智能体AI治理分为四个独立但耦合的层次（个体控制、群体涌现、人机协同、集群运维），通过理论形式化、跨层耦合分析和实证研究，揭示生产环境中82%的智能体故障为多层级问题，发现现有工具在涌现层覆盖率不足，命名"涌现差距"，并构建五级成熟度模型以应对欧盟AI法案第14条的人类监督法律要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI智能体的速度超过其治理能力，现有方法将单一学科（通常为面向确定性自动化的DevSecOps）延伸到所有代理尺度，导致治理不足。智能体AI治理被误解为单一问题而非四个具有独立治理科学的层次，造成实践与能力之间的根本性错配。

Method: 提出CASE框架：将控制理论应用于个体智能体（意图作为设定值、护栏作为反馈、评估作为观测），复杂自适应系统理论应用于智能体群体（涌现使单智能体保证不可组合），监督控制论应用于人机团队（必要性多样性定律表明无辅助人类监督在结构上失败），工程运维应用于智能体集群（将错误预算扩展到决策质量）。形式化每一层次，推导跨层耦合条件（包括零接触部署悖论），追踪二十多个企业控制措施到其经典构念，并通过三项实证研究验证。

Result: 三项实证研究发现：82%的已文档化生产智能体故障为多层级轨迹；22个生态系统工具中没有一个提供完整的第2层（涌现）覆盖；35个评分的公开部署均处于最低成熟度层级。命名此错配为"涌现差距"（Emergence Gap）——在涌现层实现的风险与几乎未提供的能力以及缺失的实践之间的差距。

Conclusion: 构建五级成熟度模型，带有非补偿性瓶颈加权指数和评估工具，将CASE操作化为基于生产企业智能体平台的科学成熟度模型而非流程成熟度模型。鉴于欧盟AI法案第14条将有效人类监督作为法律要求，只有满足必要性多样性的架构才能使监督真实而非形式化。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [244] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出SBCO（自监督块坐标优化器），一种基于验证器的优化框架，通过自监督而非自指的方式实现智能体改进。在两个任务领域上，SBCO匹配或超越了自修改基线方法，同时计算开销降低4-5.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自改进方法（如Darwin Gödel Machine）依赖于自指式自我改进，即智能体编辑自身代码。这种方法要求任务能力与自我修改能力高度对齐，适用于编程任务。对于不满足这种对齐的其他任务，传统方法需要移除自指特性或引入显式的元智能体自我修改，计算成本高昂，需要大量候选智能体的搜索。因此，需要一种更低成本的替代方案。

Method: 提出SBCO（Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer），这是一种基于验证器的harness优化器，属于与Gödel机器方法相同的闭环、经验改进类，但采用自监督而非自指。SBCO通过近似块坐标上升学习分解的验证器库和harness策略，利用自身反馈信号不断改进智能体输出，固定元智能体且无需人工标签。

Result: 在两个任务领域进行评估，SBCO匹配或超越了定制的自修改基线方法，同时计算预算降低4-5.5倍。

Conclusion: SBCO为显式约束的规划任务提供了一种低成本的自改进替代方案，通过自监督学习验证器和优化策略，实现高效智能体改进，避免自指方法的能力对齐限制和高昂计算开销。

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>


### [245] [Generating Attacks for LLMs with GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10171)
*Berkay Ozcam,Irem Onen,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali,Emin Islam Tatli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GFlowNets的自动化红队测试框架，通过训练一个LLM作为攻击者来测试另一个LLM（受害者模型）的安全漏洞，实现无需人工干预的自适应对抗攻击生成，并提供模型鲁棒性的量化评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的广泛应用引发了显著的安全漏洞问题。现有红队测试方法存在局限：人工测试耗时长，自动化方法依赖固定数据集导致创造力不足。因此需要一种高效、自适应的自动化方法来更有效地识别和评估LLM安全漏洞。

Method: 采用GFlowNets（流匹配网络）构建攻击者模型，针对指定的受害者模型进行训练，实现自动化红队测试。该框架利用一个LLM生成对抗性输入攻击另一个LLM，通过强化学习优化攻击策略，并输出量化鲁棒性评分。方法具备自适应性，无需人工参与即可持续探索新的攻击模式。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在英语场景下生成的对抗攻击比现有基准更有效。此外，论文做出了创新性贡献，首次实现了土耳其语攻击输入的生成能力，扩展了多语言安全测试的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于GFlowNets的自动化红队测试方法为LLM安全评估提供了高效解决方案，能够自适应地生成多样化对抗攻击，有效发现模型安全漏洞并提供定量鲁棒性评估。该方法不仅提升了英语攻击效果，还拓展至土耳其语等低资源语言，具有重要的理论意义和实用价值。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [246] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)
*Touseef Hasan,Laila Cure,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，通过改进检索质量来增强公共服务对话系统的约束感知推荐能力，实验证明检索质量比模型大小更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务聊天机器人需要基于公共服务目录提供符合用户约束的推荐，但现有目录存在噪声和不一致性，通用LLM容易产生不可靠推荐并引用未验证的网络来源。需要解决检索质量对约束感知推荐的影响问题。

Method: 提出TRACE（Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine）框架，基于检索的约束感知框架，通过双数据表示模式将用户查询解析为结构和语义约束用于下游检索。使用精心整理的全州食品柜目录和合成查询基准，评估多种知识表示变体（含知识图谱），实验多种开源LLM和专有模型。

Result: 增强检索能力显著提高了用户约束满足率，减少了幻觉推荐。随着检索质量提升，不同LLM之间的性能差异缩小，结果对模型大小的敏感度降低。

Conclusion: 检索质量是构建稳健公共服务对话系统的关键因素，比单纯的模型规模调整更为重要。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [247] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文表明Vision Wormhole的潜在空间通信具有高度压缩性。通过使用稀疏自编码器对冻结激活进行后验拟合，构建k=4的稀疏载荷可将传输字节数减少128倍，而七项非AIME任务平均准确率仅从49.85%微降至49.77%。4096元素的字典仅使用50个特征，任务级激活集的平均Jaccard相似度为0.906。研究证实了强压缩性，但未完全分离稀疏编码的独立贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Wormhole采用固定容量稠密张量在不同异构视觉-语言模型代理间传输视觉特征，但该张量可能仅使用少量表征自由度。这种冗余提示通信信道可能具有显著压缩潜力，激发对稀疏后验编码可行性的研究动机。

Method: 对冻结的Vision Wormhole激活拟合后验稀疏自编码器，在九个推理基准上评估重建质量、下游效用、特征复用和token级干预。比较uint16索引/float16值稀疏载荷（每token k=4活跃系数）与原始float32传输方案。

Result: 稀疏载荷将传输字节数减少128倍；七项非AIME任务平均准确率变化极小（49.85%→49.77%）；4096元素字典仅使用50个特征；任务级激活集平均Jaccard相似度为0.906。

Conclusion: 实验证实了相对于原始传输的强后验压缩性，但未能完全分离稀疏编码相对于位置选择、精度降低、低秩结构或SAE优化效应的增量贡献。结果激励匹配载荷比较和根据每条消息信息量自适应调整通信机制。
